### 2026-10-24: Dev, DJ stage 2a (Export to Traktor)

Built on `feat/dj-traktor-export` from `integration` (7bd652b4), from `DJ area (Traktor, decks).dc.html` 21a and 21b (Phase D, stage 2a; direction approved by Ross 24 October), BACKLOG "DJ area" stage 2 (a), and Dev's answer 160 for host paths. **Gauntlet:** `core/dj/traktor.py` writes files into a configured folder and `api/dj.py` gains a settings write and a file route, so both are now on `.github/gauntlet-paths.txt` and this branch goes through the gauntlet; the lead arranges the independent review. Semgrep's `use-defused-xml` flagged the writer's `import xml.etree.ElementTree`: the module only **builds** XML and never parses anything (defusedxml has no builder), so the import carries `# nosemgrep` with that reason on the line above. Semgrep also listed `nan-injection` on `api/dj.py`'s `_float` (the BPM filter's reader, from stage 1); since `api/dj.py` is now a risky path and a full scan judges every line, `_float` refuses "nan" and "inf" in any case before the cast (a NaN already read as no filter; the test is `test_a_bpm_filter_of_nan_is_ignored`). The new `within` in the export body only takes a finite JSON number. Routes: `GET`/`PUT /api/library/dj/traktor/settings`, `POST /api/library/dj/traktor/export` (`CONTRACTS.md`, "Export to Traktor"). Patterns: `DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` (Export to Traktor…, Tracks' selection, the Traktor card, `SelectionBar primaryVariant`); `SCREENS.md` gains two rows; `docs/GAUNTLET.md` the four scenarios.

**What was built**

- **The writer** (`core/dj/traktor.py`): a Traktor Pro 3 playlist file, `NML VERSION="19"`, built with ElementTree (every attribute escaped; characters XML 1.0 can't hold are dropped first): `HEAD`, `MUSICFOLDERS`, a `COLLECTION` with each track once (`ENTRY TITLE ARTIST` > `LOCATION VOLUME DIR FILE`, `ALBUM TITLE`, `INFO BITRATE PLAYTIME PLAYTIME_FLOAT KEY`, `TEMPO BPM`, `MUSICAL_KEY VALUE`), and `PLAYLISTS` > `NODE FOLDER $ROOT` > `SUBNODES` > `NODE PLAYLIST` > `PLAYLIST LIST UUID` > `ENTRY` > `PRIMARYKEY TRACK KEY` in order. Never cue points, grids (`CUE_V2`), ratings or `LOCK`. The key (both `MUSICAL_KEY` and `INFO KEY`) only for a tag's key or analysis at 0.5 confidence or more; BPM for 20–400.
- **Mac paths:** `MacPaths.mac_path_for` asks the drive helper's `core.drives.host_paths.to_host` first (`_load_to_host`: the one import to swap once `feat/host-paths` lands; until then it finds nothing and the setting stands alone), then maps a file under the library folder (`soulseek.transfer_path`) onto the card's "Your music on the Mac"; a media server's stored path is resolved to its file first (`resolve_track_file`); a path already under `/Volumes/` or `/Users/` is used as it is (answer 160's `/Volumes:/Volumes` case). A track nothing can place is **left out with its reason**.
- **The three doors (21a):** a playlist's "…" (`routes/sync/-ui/mirrored-detail-modal.tsx`, before the hairline and Delete; user playlists and mirrors alike), Tracks' selection bar (Tracks gains Select / Done, checkboxes in the art cell, 7c's `SelectionBar` with "Select all N shown", its "Export to Traktor…" a secondary), and the Mixes panel's foot ("Make a playlist…" and "Export to Traktor…" side by side, the phone sheet too; it exports the seed and up to 200 matches). All open `TraktorExportDialog`.
- **The confirm:** tiles (Tracks, Found on your Mac, Left out), "Paths as your Mac sees them" with the first track's path and "Change…" (admins), the Left out box with one sentence per reason, "Saves **Sunday Set.nml** to your Downloads. In Traktor: **File → Import Playlist…** (or right-click Playlists → Import Playlist). The BPM and key go in the file; Traktor keeps any it already has.", the note "Traktor Pro 3's playlist format (.nml)", Cancel and "Export 23 tracks".
- **Delivery:** the browser downloads the file (`Content-Disposition: attachment` with an ASCII name and `filename*`; `nosniff`, `no-store`). With an export folder set and usable, an admin's export saves there instead (a member always downloads), with Download instead beside it; a file already there is never replaced without **Replace it**. The folder must be a writable directory inside the library, Downloads or a music folder once links are followed, checked when it's set and again at every write; the file goes to a `mkstemp` name in the folder, then is hard-linked into place (which refuses an existing name or link), or renamed over it once Replace it is pressed. In Docker without Traktor's folder mounted, the download is the default (no folder set).
- **Settings → Library → Traktor Pro 3 (21b):** after "BPM, key and energy", mounted like the Folders card: Your music on the Mac, Export folder (optional), Traktor's collection (informational). Admins change and Save (`PUT`, admin-only); members read. Stored as `library.traktor_mac_path` and `library.traktor_export_folder` through `config_manager.set` (the Settings page's save writes leaves, so it never clears them).
- **Profile scope:** every track lookup goes through the DJ store's `_scope`; a playlist that isn't the caller's reads as missing (admins read any, as the mirrored-playlist routes do), and its rows only match tracks in the caller's library. Input: at most 1,000 track ids (400 beyond), a 64 KB body (413), `action` and `source` checked.

**The NML format: confirmed and assumed**

Confirmed (a real Traktor `collection.nml` in dj-data-converter's test resources, and the readers in Mixxx `traktorfeature.cpp`, trak2rek and dj-data-converter; read, none of their code used): the element and attribute names above; `DIR` as `/:`-separated folders ending `/:`; `PRIMARYKEY KEY` = `VOLUME + DIR + FILE`; on a Mac `VOLUME` is the volume's name (Mixxx rebuilds `/Volumes/<VOLUME><DIR><FILE>`); the playlist tree `$ROOT` > `SUBNODES` > `NODE TYPE="PLAYLIST"` > `PLAYLIST` > `ENTRY` > `PRIMARYKEY TYPE="TRACK"`; **`MUSICAL_KEY VALUE` 0–23: 0–11 the major keys from C up by semitone, 12–23 the minor keys the same way** (the real file pairs `VALUE="7"` with `INFO KEY="2d"`, G major; trak2rek reads `KEYS[v % 12] + ("m" if v >= 12)`; Mixxx's comment says Traktor uses its numbering, but Mixxx counts from 1 with 0 invalid, which the real file contradicts, so 0-based it is); `INFO KEY` holds Open Key text.

Assumed (no Traktor in this environment; **Ross checks on his Mac**): that Traktor resolves a `LOCATION` without `VOLUMEID` (a volume serial SYNCHRO.S can't know) and without `MODIFIED_DATE`, `AUDIO_ID` or `BPM_QUALITY`; that the start-up disk is `VOLUME="Macintosh HD"` for a `/Users/…` path (macOS's default name; a renamed disk would need the helper to name it); that Import Playlist adds a track Traktor doesn't have with this file's BPM and key, and keeps its own for one it already has; that `NML VERSION="19"` is accepted by every Traktor Pro 3.x.

**Key mapping** (`traktor_key_value`, tested for all 24): Camelot 8B C = 0, 3B D♭ = 1, 10B D = 2, 5B E♭ = 3, 12B E = 4, 7B F = 5, 2B F♯ = 6, 9B G = 7, 4B A♭ = 8, 11B A = 9, 6B B♭ = 10, 1B B = 11; 5A Cm = 12, 12A C♯m = 13, 7A Dm = 14, 2A E♭m = 15, 9A Em = 16, 4A Fm = 17, 11A F♯m = 18, 6A Gm = 19, 1A G♯m = 20, 8A Am = 21, 3A B♭m = 22, 10A Bm = 23. Open Key = Camelot − 7 (mod 12), m for A and d for B (8A = 1m).

**After the gauntlet review (of 32fa2c75)**

The independent review listed one Medium and six Lows; each is changed as below, with a regression test that fails on 32fa2c75 (those marked *gauntlet* carry the marker).

1. *Looking stored paths up on disk (gauntlet).* A stored path outside the library folder was looked up on disk for every track, and a lookup can ask the media server where its music lives. Now the lookup is skipped when nothing could place the result (no Mac path, no helper map, and no `/Volumes` or `/Users` in the container); one export looks up at most 50 paths or for at most 5 s, and the rest are left out as `lookup_limit` ("…weren't looked for on disk: too many to look up in one export. Export them in smaller sets."); a path that resolved to nothing isn't looked up again for 5 minutes (at most 5,000 remembered).
2. *The export folder between the check and the write (gauntlet).* The folder is opened once (`O_DIRECTORY | O_NOFOLLOW`), `fstat`ed against the device and inode noted at the check (and, where `/proc/self/fd` says where it is, checked to be inside a configured folder), and every step after that (the temporary file, link, rename, unlink) is relative to that open folder.
3. *The file's mode.* The saved file is `0644` less the umask (read from `/proc/self/status`, `022` where it can't be) on both paths; it was `0600` after a hard link.
4. *Orphaned temporary files and the no-link path (gauntlet).* Each save sweeps `.synchros-traktor-*.tmp` older than an hour. Without hard links the file is written under a temporary name, the final name is claimed with an exclusive create, and the written file is renamed over the claim; any failure removes the claim and the temporary file. **This path isn't atomic:** for the moment between the claim and the rename the name holds an empty file.
5. *What members see.* `GET /settings` gives members whether the Mac path is set (`mac_path_set`, `mac_path_from_helper_set`) and the export folder's state, not the paths; a member's preview keeps `example_path` (their own track) and drops `mac_path`, `library_folder` and the folder's name. The card shows members "Set by an admin" or "Not set yet".
6. *Chunked bodies.* The export and settings routes read at most 64 KB + 1 byte of the body, with or without a Content-Length, and answer 413 past it.
7. *File names.* Unicode format characters (`Cf`, such as the right-to-left override and zero-width characters) are dropped, and a name that would be Traktor's own `collection.nml` becomes `collection (playlist).nml`.

`feat/host-paths` (`to_host`) isn't merged yet; `_load_to_host` stays the one import to swap.

**How Ross tests it in Traktor Pro 3**

1. Settings → Library → Traktor Pro 3: type where the Mac keeps the library folder (the left side of `./music:/app/Transfer` in the compose file, e.g. `/Users/ross/SoulSync/music`, or `/Volumes/Music HD/…` after the move) and Save.
2. Open a playlist's "…" → Export to Traktor…; check the path shown is the one Finder shows for that file; Export. `Sunday Set.nml` lands in Downloads.
3. In Traktor: File → Import Playlist… (or right-click Playlists → Import Playlist) and pick the file. The playlist should appear under Playlists with every track playable (no "file missing" icon).
4. Check a track Traktor didn't have before: its BPM and Key columns are filled (Key in your chosen notation; an uncertain SYNCHRO.S key is left blank on purpose).
5. Check a track Traktor already had: its own BPM, key, grid and cues are unchanged.
6. Try one from Tracks (Select, tick a few, Export to Traktor…) and one from the Mixes panel; and, if the files are on Music HD, one with `/Volumes/Music HD/…`.
7. If tracks show as missing, say which form the path took (`/Users/…` or `/Volumes/…`) and whether the start-up disk has another name than Macintosh HD.

**Decided by Dev**

1. One route with `action: preview | download | save` rather than three; the file is built again for the download (a preview isn't stored), so nothing sits on the server between the two.
2. Saving into the export folder is admin-only; members download. The folder is checked when set and at every write.
3. The Mixes export takes the seed plus up to 200 matches (`mixes_with`'s limit), not only the 50 the panel shows.
4. A selection's file is named "SYNCHRO.S tracks YYYY-MM-DD"; a playlist's by its name; Mixes "Mixes with <title>".
5. Playlist rows match library tracks by title and artist (case-insensitive), as Play does (`resolve_playable_tracks`), but within the caller's library scope.
6. Tracks' selection keeps to loaded rows ("Select all 50 shown"), not the whole filter.

**Tests:** Python: `tests/dj/test_traktor.py` (28: every key both ways, locations, Mac paths with the helper first and the resolver second, file names, the file read back, left-out reasons; the routes: preview and download, a hostile name's Content-Disposition, no Mac path, input checks, a profile needed, Mixes, a playlist, settings admin-only and checked, saving with 409 and Replace; **gauntlet**: XML injection, an escaping folder, no overwrite, profile scope, a stored folder swapped for a link). Run with `tests/dj` (182 passed), `pytest -m gauntlet`, every test that reads an edited file; `ruff check` clean. Web: `npm run check` (0 errors), `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`, the full vitest suite with `--testTimeout=60000`: 654 files, 11,667 tests, all pass (CI's webui job on 6069677a; this sandbox's own full run stalled under two other agents' suites and was stopped, and the touched areas, `features/dj`, `routes/library`, `routes/sync` and `app`, pass here: 114 files, 2,470 tests). The first CI run caught the card's settings arriving after a test's teardown (an unhandled rejection); fixed. New: `features/dj/traktor.test.tsx` (the words, the confirm's parts, download, nothing found, the folder and Replace it, Try again, the card for admins and members, its mount); `tracks-page.test.tsx` (selection and the bar's export); `mirrored-detail-modal.test.tsx` (the menu's order and the confirm); `mixes-panel.test.tsx` (the foot's two buttons and the Mixes export).

**Screenshots** (1440×900 and 390×844, in the session scratchpad's `shots-d2a/` beside the board renders `board-21a.png` and `board-21b.png`; not in the repo): the playlist's "…" with Export to Traktor… and its confirm with one track left out (1440; the confirm at 390), Tracks in selection with three ticked and the bar, and its confirm (1440, 390), the Mixes panel's foot and its confirm (1440), the Mixes sheet's foot (390), Settings → Library → Traktor Pro 3 (1440, 390). The real app on a scratch server with a seeded library and a 24-track "Sunday Set" (one row not in the library); the built shell loads with no console errors.

### 2026-10-24: Dev, phase C2 (every Connections service modal on 15a's frame)

Built on `ux/oct24-c2` from `integration` (7bd652b4), from Phase C of Design's "Build order for Dev, 24 October": answer 132, "every service modal takes 15a's frame and footer", with board 15a (`Answers 83-95.dc.html`) and phase A's Spotify modal ("Dev, phase A (Design's answers 83–95)" below) as the model. No routes changed and nothing new asks a service anything: the tags and lines come from answers the page already reads (`/status`, `/api/settings/config-status`, the settings answer, `/api/qobuz/auth/status`) and from Test connection's own answer. **Nothing on `.github/gauntlet-paths.txt` was touched** (web files only), so this branch needs no gauntlet review.

**What changed**

- **One frame for every service in Settings → Connections**, music and video: Spotify, iTunes / Apple Music, Deezer, Discogs, Tidal, Qobuz, Hydrabase, MusicBrainz, Last.fm, ListenBrainz, Genius, Concerts, AcoustID; TMDB, TVDB, Community Data, OMDb, Trakt, MDBList, OpenSubtitles, SubDL, SubSource, fanart.tv, YouTube Extras. Phase A's Spotify-only code (`renderStgSpotifyModal`'s head and footer, the CSS scoped to `[data-svc="spotify"]`) became `renderServiceModalFrame()`, drawn from a pure `serviceFrameView(id, ctx)` over `_svcFrameContext(id, frame)`, and the CSS now applies to `#service-config-modal` for all of them. The legacy forms are still moved in untouched; only the form's own Test Connection and Disconnect buttons are marked (`data-svc-foot`) and hidden in the modal, because the footer runs the same calls (`testConnection('<id>')`, Deezer's `testDeezerConnection()` for both its logins, a video service's `data-video-test-service` list; `disconnectSpotify`, `disconnectTidal`, `logoutQobuz`). A row of actions left empty by that hides with them.
- **The head**: the 40px logo well, the name at 22/600, a StatusTag beside it where the service reports a state, and one line under them that says who it's connected as and what it's used for. No line where there's nothing to say ("No Key" is left out: the tile already says "no key needed").

  | Service | Line | Tag (no call) |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | Spotify | "Connected as **ross** · 14 playlists · metadata source" (as phase A) | the pause ("Paused by Spotify · 11 min", "Easing back in · 5 min"), else Connected, else Not connected once a client id is in |
  | Tidal | "Playlists & Metadata" | Connected (config-status: it holds a token), else Not connected once a client id is in |
  | Qobuz | "Connected as **Ross** · Studio" (the name and plan Qobuz handed back at sign-in, kept by the server; read when the modal opens, as the Sources tab does) | Connected / Not connected |
  | Last.fm | "Connected as **ross** · scrobbling your plays" once Last.fm has handed back a session | Connected; **Not authorized** (warning) while scrobbling is on without a session |
  | Deezer, OMDb, Trakt, MDBList, the subtitle services, fanart.tv | the service's own line ("Favorites & Playlists", "Ratings", "Subtitles"…), plus "· metadata source" when it's the primary source | after Test only |
  | the rest | none | after Test only |

- **After Test connection** the answer becomes the tag, on the tile too, for this page: **Connected** (success), **Needs a look** (warning: it worked with a warning, e.g. Deezer's expired login), **Rate-limited** (warning), **Refused** (danger: invalid, unauthorised, expired), **Can't reach** (danger), **Not set up** (neutral: a key is missing), **Test failed** (danger, anything else). A passed test never says "Connected" for a service with an account that isn't connected (Spotify's test passes on the fallback source). Changing a field in the modal, Save or Disconnect clears it.
- **The warning box** tops the body for that service's problems, 10px radius, the tone at 35% border and 7% fill, a bold lead and the sentence: a failed or warned test ("The service refused what it was given. Invalid Discogs personal token."), Last.fm's missing authorisation, an empty required video key ("Required. Nothing on the video side works until this key is set."). Spotify's pause box stays as built.
- **The footer** on every service: Disconnect as a ghost on the left only while connected, and only for the three that had a Disconnect (Spotify, Tidal, Qobuz); Test connection (secondary) when the form had a test; Save (primary; the video side saves through the page's Save button, which it takes over). Hydrabase keeps its Connect in the form (it opens the socket and says so), so its footer is Save alone. The footer is built once per service, so a re-render (Spotify's countdown) keeps the focus.
- **Tiles**: each tile carries the same StatusTag in place of its chip where the service reports a state (the Spotify tile now reads "Connected" when there's no pause, as 15a's note says); a service that reports nothing keeps its chip ("configured", "not set up", "no key needed", "required").
- **No key or token reaches the frame.** It reads only named fields (an account's name and playlist count, Qobuz's name and plan, whether Last.fm has a session), checks inputs only for being empty, and the sentence from a test's answer has any `key=`/`token=` value in a URL and any 32-character token replaced by "…" before it's shown. Every answer it reads was checked: `/status`'s `spotify` (`account` is `{name, playlists}`), `metadata_source` and `enrichment` (booleans and counts), config-status (booleans), the settings answer (secrets masked), Qobuz's (`display_name`, `subscription`).

**Decided by Dev**

1. Plex, Jellyfin and Navidrome aren't service modals: they're Connections' "Server Connections" picker with forms in place; slskd and YouTube are on the Sources tab, in its own modal. Both left as they are (question 220).
2. Tags that need a call show only after Test, and only for this page's life (question 222).
3. Test connection in the footer runs exactly what the form's button ran; the button stays in the form (hidden in the modal) so the legacy list and the tests that read it still find it.

**Tests:** Web: `npm run check` (0 errors), `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`; the full vitest suite with `--testTimeout=60000` (654 files, 11,668 tests; 11,663 passed and 5 failed under load in files this phase doesn't touch: `module-gate.test.tsx`, `-sync.use-pipeline.test.tsx`'s 2.5 s tick and three in `wishlist-audiobooks.test.tsx`, all 29 passing when those files run again on their own). New: `src/test/settings-service-frame.test.ts` (15: what each test answer becomes, keys and tokens masked, each service's head, tag, line and footer, Last.fm's warning, the required video key, a token in a status answer never reaching the frame, Discogs' Test → Refused and the box → cleared, Deezer's two-login test, Hydrabase's Save-only footer, a video service's test list and Save, Tidal's Disconnect then config-status, the footer kept across re-renders, and that the marked form buttons run the footer's calls), and the Spotify tests moved onto the shared harness (`src/test/service-frame-harness.ts`, 7, plus "a passed test never says Connected while no account is"). Python: every test that reads `index.html`, `settings.js` or `style.css`, 116 files, 2,551 passed.

**Screenshots** (session scratchpad `shots-c2/`, not in the repo; the real app on a scratch server with the account states and test answers stood in, since the sandbox reaches no service): every service modal at 1440×900 (`music-<id>-1440`, `video-<id>-1440`), Spotify paused (`music-spotify-paused-1440`), the grids after Test (`music-grid-after-tests-1440`, `video-grid-after-tests-1440`) and with Spotify paused; at 390×844 Spotify paused, Discogs refused and Qobuz connected (each with its `-end` scrolled to the footer). The built shell's console is clean on Settings and through every screenshot.

### 2026-10-24: Dev, phase B3 (MilkDrop's curated list, the drive move's slskd choice and Windows steps)

Built on `ux/oct24-b3` from `integration` (f492a490), from Phase B of Design's "Build order for Dev, 24 October" (Ross said yes to both parts) and the boards `Answers 100-134.dc.html` 18a, 18b, 19a and 19b, with Design's answers 100–106 and 120–125. The drive-move part changes `core/drives/move.py`, so it went through the gauntlet; the lead arranges the independent review.

**Per item**

- **101 / 18a, MilkDrop lists the curated 395 by default.** The build (`webui/scripts/milkdrop-presets.mjs`) marks each bundled preset `curated` from `butterchurn-presets`' own pack meta (`lib/butterchurnPresetPackMeta.min.js`): its main pack, Extra, Extra 2 and MD1, 395 presets (one of them is named "….milk" in the meta, matched without it). `GET /api/visuals/presets` takes **`set=curated|all`** (curated when left out; anything else is 400 `bad_set`) and answers `curated` / `uncurated` counts; in the curated set the profile's favourites and the presets Recent names (`keep=`) that it leaves out come back as **`kept`**, so Favourites and Recent keep what's in them, switch or not. The panel's foot gains **"Every preset"** first ("Adds 1,359 more, uncurated: some names are crude, some won't load.", a `LabelledSwitch`, remembered in this browser, question 200), which asks for `set=all`; a switch while the list is on its way asks again. Show gains **"Yours N"** once a folder is set (not shown without one); the count line reads "395 from the curated packs and 12 of yours · A–Z" ("… from every pack …" with the switch on, "12 of yours · A–Z" on Yours; filtered lists keep "n presets · A–Z · n authors"); the search placeholder counts what's listed. With 2.4.7 the numbers are 395 and 1,359 (the board's 1,361 counted two converted files the build doesn't copy). Code: `core/visuals/presets.py` `build_index`, `api/visuals.py`, `features/visuals/` (`visuals-controller.ts` `setEveryPreset`, `milkdrop-browser.tsx`, `visuals-panel.tsx`, `visuals.logic.ts` `filterPresets`/`withKept`/`browserSummary`, `visuals-storage.ts`).
- **103 / 18b, Player settings → MilkDrop → Your own presets.** "Your own presets" over "A folder of .milk files on the server. They join MilkDrop's list for everyone on this SYNCHRO.S, under Yours.", the folder and Save, then a line with an 8px dot, in words: read (success) "12 presets, read 2 minutes ago. 1 is too big (over 512 KB) and was left out."; empty (warning) "The folder has no .milk files yet. Put them in it; SYNCHRO.S reads it again when MilkDrop next opens."; unreadable (danger) "SYNCHRO.S can't open /config/milkdrop: it isn't there inside the container. Check the folder is in your set-up's volumes." (or "…it isn't allowed to read it…" when it's there but can't be read). The listing now counts `too_big` and says when it read (`read_at`). Yours' empty state is a quiet card with **Player settings** as an inline link that closes the overlay and opens the tray's Player settings (question 201). Code: `visuals.logic.ts` `ownFolderLine`, `visuals-host.tsx`, `visuals-stage-cards.tsx` `openPlayerSettings`.
- **120 / 19a, Check's slskd row.** Moving Downloads needs slskd's sign-in, and the row now asks for the choice instead of stopping: "slskd downloads to the new place", the why in one sentence ("To download into Music HD, slskd has to restart, and it only lets a signed-in admin do that. SYNCHRO.S signs in once, keeps only what slskd hands back, and forgets it when the move is done."), the warning tag **"Needs your choice"** (`needs_choice`, not `stop`: `ok` stays false, so the plan still waits), and two option cards in a `radiogroup`: **"Move Downloads too: sign in to slskd"** (the default, picked on Choose; it holds User name and Password and "Sign in to slskd") and **"Leave Downloads on Macintosh HD"** (`PUT …/choice {downloads_left: true}`: Downloads leaves this move, any slskd session is forgotten at once, Check runs again). The sign-in is the existing one-time session (`POST …/slskd-session`): only the token slskd hands back is kept, encrypted, and forgotten after the switch, when a draft is left and now when Downloads leaves the move; the password is cleared from the page once sent. A refused sign-in is `400 code: "slskd_refused"` and shows under Password. Signed in, the row reads "Signed in as slskd" (the name is kept in memory with the move, never on disk; after a restart it reads "Signed in"). See the plan stays disabled-neutral with "Sign in to slskd, or leave Downloads where it is, to see the plan." (question 202). Code: `core/drives/move.py` `set_choice`, `_slskd_choice_row`, `slskd_sign_in`; `features/drives/move-steps.tsx` `SlskdChoice`.
- **122 / 19b, the Docker step on Windows.** Three numbered cards by hand, until the PowerShell helper is tested: **Keep a copy of your set-up file** (File Explorer, the install folder, paste a copy beside it); **Add the lines in Notepad** (the file's lines with the added ones in green, at the file's indentation: the drive's line under `soulsync:` and `slskd:`, and slskd's settings folder under `soulsync:`; "Copy the line"; the drive letter as a select, D by default); **Restart SYNCHRO.S from PowerShell** (`Set-Location -LiteralPath 'C:\SoulSync'; if ($?) { docker compose up -d }` and Copy). Then "Changed your mind? Put the copy from step 1 back and run step 3 again. A one-paste helper for Windows is on its way.", and the footer "Looking every 3 s for SYNCHRO.S to restart" with Check again (the page already looked by itself). The lead says "Once, on this computer. Three short steps in File Explorer, Notepad and PowerShell."; the helper's success note and the old neutral Notice are gone on Windows (question 203). Code: `features/drives/docker-step.tsx` `WindowsSteps`, `windowsComposeLines`, `windowsRestartCommand`.

**Routes**

- `GET /api/visuals/presets` gains `set=curated|all` (default `curated`) and `keep=`; the answer gains `set`, `kept`, `curated`, `uncurated`, each preset's `curated`, and `own.too_big` / `own.read_at` (also in `GET /api/visuals/settings`). CONTRACTS.md, "Visuals".
- `PUT /api/drives/move/<id>/choice` takes `downloads_left`; the slskd check row gains `slskd_choice`, `signed_in`, `home_label`, `target_label`; `POST …/slskd-session` refuses with `code: "slskd_refused"`. CONTRACTS.md, "Your music's drives".

**Components changed**

- The move flow's footer (`move.module.css` `.footEnd`): a disabled action's reason reads **before** the button, as 19a draws it (it was after).
- Option cards in a check row gain a second form: a card whose head is the `radio` and which holds fields (`.optionCard`, `.optionHead`, `.optionFields`; DESIGN-SYSTEM.md). The old inline sign-in row (`.signIn`) is gone.
- Code boxes in the flow wrap on a phone: Copy goes under the code.
- The Visuals panel gains an inline link style (`.inlineLink`, accent-light, underline on hover) and the quiet Yours card.

**Decided by Dev**

1. **Curated means the four packs `butterchurn-presets` curates** (main, Extra, Extra 2, MD1), read from the package at build time, not a list kept here; an index built before the mark counts every preset as curated, so an old build never lists nothing.
2. **Favourites and Recent keep presets outside the curated list** through `kept` (the server adds the profile's stars; the browser names Recent's), rather than fetching every preset anyway. V and auto-cycle walk what's listed.
3. **"Every preset" per browser** (Ross's brief), question 200.
4. **Leaving Downloads takes it out of the move** (the same as unticking it on Choose) and forgets the slskd session straight away; re-ticking on Choose is a fresh choice and resets the row's.
5. **The slskd user name for "Signed in as …" lives in memory only**, beside the move; nothing new is stored, and the password and token are never in an answer or the log.
6. **Windows shows three lines, an install-folder field and a letter select**, so the line and the command are exact for that computer (question 203).

**After the independent gauntlet review** (fixes on the same branch; each with a test that failed before it):

1. **A folder that stays may not sit inside one that moves.** Downloads (or Staging) nested in the Library and left out of a move was copied with the Library, and Remove the old copies would then delete files SYNCHRO.S still downloaded into. Now: Check refuses with a stop row and the choice's place is refused with `400 nested_folder` ("…Move Downloads too, or move it out of the Library folder first."); Start's walk leaves out any folder that stays; Remove skips anything inside a folder still in use and doesn't tidy empty folders around it. Gauntlet tests for Downloads and Staging.
2. **A choice and Start can't cross.** Choices and the slskd sign-in are written under the move's lock and only while the move is a draft (a conditional write); Start re-reads the move after listing the files and stops cleanly if the choice, plan or update time changed. A failed move can be closed with Leave (as Stop and undo), and carrying on refuses a move without a plan (`409 no_plan`). Gauntlet tests for a write during the walk and a choice sent while a move runs.
3. **slskd's session only while Downloads moves.** After slskd answers, the move is read again under the lock: the session is kept only if it's still a draft with Downloads ticked (otherwise `409 slskd_not_needed`). A failed move forgets the session; carrying on (Carry on, Copy them again, Carry on without them, Try the switch again) asks for slskd's sign-in again when Downloads moves (`409 slskd_sign_in`, then the fields under the failure's note). Gauntlet tests.
4. **The Windows restart command is quoted:** `Set-Location -LiteralPath '<folder>'; if ($?) { docker compose up -d }` (a `'` doubled), so spaces and `(x86)` work and Compose never runs in another folder; the install-folder field keeps only letters, digits, spaces, `_ - . ( )`, `:` and `\`.
5. **The own-presets walk stays cheap:** it stops after 50,000 folder entries of any kind, resolves only folders and `.milk` files, walks again for an unknown key at most once every 5 seconds, and callers at the same moment share one walk.
6. **After the re-review:** carrying on checks the plan and that the drive is there before it signs in to slskd, so a refused carry-on never keeps a session; Leave on a draft writes only while the move is still a draft (otherwise "The move has moved on…"); the guards in Start's walk and in Remove also recognise a folder that stays when it's reached through another path to the same folder on disk (a bind mount), as `overlaps()` does.

**Tests:** Python: `tests/drives`, `tests/visuals`, `tests/dj/test_review_fixes.py`, `tests/dj/test_store_and_worker.py`, `tests/webui` and `tests/test_slskd_share_rescan.py` (396 passed) and `pytest -m gauntlet` (42 passed); `ruff check` clean. New: the curated set, `all`, `bad_set`, `kept` from favourites and `keep` (junk ignored, per profile), the old index counting as curated, `too_big` and `read_at`, empty / missing / unreadable folders (`test_visuals_api.py`); the slskd row's choice, refused sign-in code, nothing secret in `public()`, leaving Downloads (session forgotten, row "leave", back again), Choose resetting the row, and **a gauntlet scenario: leaving Downloads never touches it or slskd's download folder** through a whole move (`test_move.py`); the routes end to end with a fake slskd, never returning the password or token (`test_paths_and_routes.py`). Web: `npm run check` (0 errors), `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`; the full vitest suite with `--testTimeout=60000`: 648 files, 11,587 tests; 11,585 passed and 2 timed out under load in files this phase doesn't touch (`artist-detail/-route.test.tsx`'s Safe-action remove, `watchlist/-route.test.tsx`'s artist settings), both passing when run again on their own (88 of 88). New: the build's curated mark and 395 from the installed package (`milkdrop-presets.test.ts`), `withKept`/`filterPresets` Yours and kept, `browserSummary`, `ownFolderLine`, `agoWords` (`visuals.logic.test.ts`), `set=curated` with `keep`, kept entries named and listed in Recent, Every preset fetching `all` and remembered, a switch mid-fetch (`visuals-controller.test.ts`), the panel's curated line, Yours, the switch, and Yours' two empty states (`visuals-panel.test.tsx`), and the slskd cards, the refused sign-in under Password, Leave's `PUT` and Check, the settled row, and the Windows lines, letter, folder and command (`drives.test.tsx`).

**Screenshots** (1440×900 and 390×844, in the session scratchpad's `shots-24b3/` beside the board renders `board-18a`, `board-18b`, `board-19a`, `board-19b`, `board-14c`; not in the repo): `18a-panel`, `18a-every`, `18a-yours`, `18b-yours-empty`, `18b-settings-read`, `-empty`, `-unreadable` (each at 1440 and 390); `19a-check-1440`/`-390`, `19a-refused-1440`, `19a-signed-1440`, `19a-left-1440`/`-390`; `19b-windows-1440`, `19b-windows-full-1440`, `19b-windows-390`. The Check shots stand in the move's answer (the sandbox has no slskd to sign in to); the rest is the real app on a scratch server. The built shell's console is clean on Home, Settings, Library (Albums, History, Tracks), Radio, Stats, Wanted and Activity, and through every screenshot.

### 2026-10-24: Dev, phase B1 (the radio tray as drawn, Recent stations per profile)

Built on `ux/oct24-b1` from `integration` (f492a490), on Ross's yes to Phase B, from `Radio tray (expanded player).dc.html` (16a–16e) and Design's answers 140–148 in `Start here - Design notes 2026-10-24.dc.html`, with Dev's answer 163 for the server list. **This branch goes through the gauntlet** (`database/**`: a new table and its migration step); the lead arranges the independent review. New routes in `CONTRACTS.md` ("Recent stations, per profile"); the tray's pattern rewritten in `DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` ("The tray in radio mode", "Radio in the player"); `SCREENS.md`'s row names the boards.

**Per item**

- **141** The list's second tab reads **"Stations"** (its id, `data-player-rtray-tab="recent"` and the panel stay), and the head carries **6a's labelled Collapse**, the music list's own button (the word, then the chevron, 16% border); the 44px icon-only Collapse and its CSS went. Heard today with its badge, Stations and Collapse fit 360px (question 180 on the chevron's side). Code: `index.html`, `static/player.css`.
- **147** The **Sleep timer** is back in radio's record, between the action and "…": music's moon button (`data-player-sleep`, the same `#ss-player-sleep-menu` and timer; `player.ts` now wires every moon). A station offers Off, 15, 30, 45 and 60 minutes, never "End of track" or the hours (`sleepStepsFor` in `player-state.ts`); an "End of track" set before a station started is cleared. Running, it reads "23 min" in the selected style, as for music. At zero the station **stops as its Stop does** (`npFireSleepTimer` in `media-player.js` calls `SoulSyncRadio.stop()`; music still pauses and keeps its queue). Nothing else came back to the menu. Code: `index.html`, `src/shell/player.ts`, `player-state.ts`, `static/media-player.js`.
- **142, 163** **Recent stations on the server, per profile.** A table shaped like `radio_favourites`, `radio_recent (profile_id, station_id, station_json, played_at, PRIMARY KEY (profile_id, station_id))` with an index on `(profile_id, played_at)`, trimmed to the profile's newest **20** on every write; `station_json` is a directory station's snapshot, so the list draws without the directory. Its own migration step, `add_radio_recent` (`atomic=False`, only adds; `MusicDatabase._add_radio_recent_table`), recorded in the ledger as `radio_recent_v1`: a second start changes nothing, and a step that can't finish is an ERROR naming it in `schema_health` and runs again next start. **`GET /api/radio/recent`** answers only the caller's profile's stations, newest first (no profile parameter exists); **`POST /api/radio/recent` `{session_id}`** counts the playing station once the caller's own session has been on for 30 s (404 `no_session` for another profile's or an ended session, 409 `too_soon` before then). The page sends it once per session after 30 s of its own playing, relayed or direct, so one path covers both modes and the server still checks the session's age. Removing a link removes its rows; deleting a profile removes its rows (the profile sweep). **In the tray:** this browser's copy in `localStorage` (per profile since the review, below; now with `played_at`) is the first paint; the server's list is read when the tray opens (at most once a minute) and after a station is counted, merged over it (`mergeRecent`: the later play and the server's words win, a station only this browser has keeps its own time) and kept as the next first paint; the newest eight show (question 184). Code: `database/music_database.py`, `core/internet_radio/store.py` (`record_recent`, `recent_rows`), `core/internet_radio/hub.py` (`played_seconds`), `api/radio.py`, `src/features/radio/radio.recent.ts`, `src/shell/player-radio.ts`.
- **144** Under the deck's row, **one thing**: the ready sentence (15b's, "Not the track you hear? Scan now listens for 10 seconds and asks AcoustID, then AudD.", which the tray didn't show before), or the one trouble line, or **the scan's card in its place**: while it runs its title over the 4px bar; found nothing or failed, its title over its line (danger when it failed), until the next named track or Scan again. Code: `player-radio.ts` `drawTray`, `radio.scan.ts` `scanReadySentence`.
- **The drift check against 16a–16e** (143, 145, 146, 148 and 167 were built in phase A and match the boards): **Live** was in the info hue; it's the **danger hue** everywhere, as 16a's note says (13/600 with a 6px dot in the bar, mini bar and sheet; 14/600 with an 8px dot in the deck and the Visuals transport). The record: the action fills the row, "…" is borderless, the readout has no box (labels in a 56px column), the artist is an accent link at 15/500 (question 181). The deck: "12:34 listened" in the mono at 72%; Stop, the stream and Scan now together at the right, 12px apart; the stream picker reads "AAC 256 · relayed" for the one playing and "MP3 128" for the others (`streamChoice`); the output is a 44px icon. The list: the rows sit on the tray with hairlines, no box; Heard rows a hairline under each; a link's row says "Your link" alone (`stationRowLine`); Play is a transparent 44px outline; the auto-add row's words wrap instead of ellipsising. The "…" menu for a station has one heading, "More for this station" (Show steps aside). The bar says "Eclectic · AAC 256" under the station's name (`barStationLine`; it said "AAC 256 kbps · Radio"). 16e's sheet already matched (its Heard count is the identified one).

**Components changed**

- The music list's Collapse is now radio's too (no radio variant).
- `.ss-player-readout--plain`: the readout without its box, for radio's record.
- `.ss-player-live-tag` takes the danger hue and a 6px dot; the deck and the Visuals transport size it up.
- The "…" menu's first heading and "Show" carry `data-player-menu-head` / `data-player-menu-show`, so a station's menu reads as 16b.

**Decided by Dev**

1. **One write path for both modes:** the page's POST after 30 s, checked against the session's age on the server, rather than the relay writing for relayed play and the page for direct.
2. **The server keeps 20, the tray shows eight** (question 184).
3. **This browser's list stays merged in**, so a station played under 30 s still shows on this device (question 185).
4. **Music keeps 6a's Collapse, readout box and outlined "…"**; radio's Collapse is music's, its readout and "…" are 16a's (questions 180, 181).
5. **The sleep timer stops a station** and the player leaves with it; the expanded state is remembered (question 182).
6. **9c's confirm words stay** (question 183).
7. **"Add" while the timer runs** in the record's row (question 186).

**Tests:** Python: `tests/database/test_radio_recent_migration.py` (fresh install: columns, key, index, ledger; a second start keeps the rows and changes nothing; the oldest release fixture upgrades; a view in the table's place fails loudly in `schema_health` and the log, leaves the ledger row unwritten, and the next start finishes once fixed) and `tests/internet_radio/test_radio_recent.py` (counted only after 30 s; newest first and once; 20 kept per profile without touching another's; a profile never reads or writes another's; an ended or unknown session isn't counted; 401 without a profile; a directory station drawn from its snapshot with the directory unreachable; a removed link and an unknown id drop out; the limit clamped); `tests/internet_radio` (with `radio_recent` in its table reset), `tests/database` (including the upgrade-from-old-releases comparison) and every test that reads a file this branch edits: 731 passed; the 94 files that read `index.html`, `player.css` or `media-player.js`: 2,340 passed. `ruff check` clean. Web: `npm run check` (0 errors), `tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean, the full vitest suite with `--testTimeout=60000`: 650 files, 11,594 tests; 11,590 passed and four route tests unrelated to the player (Artist, Wanted, Watchlist, Wishlist) timed out under the machine's load and pass on a rerun (154 of 154), and CI's webui job ran the whole suite green. New: `src/features/radio/radio.recent.test.ts` (`mergeRecent`, `serverRecent`, `keepRecentStations`, `played_at`, `streamChoice`, `stationRowLine`, `scanReadySentence`), `src/test/media-player-sleep-radio.test.ts` (a station stops at zero, music pauses), `player-state.test.ts` (`sleepStepsFor`), `player-radio.tray.test.ts` ("Stations" and the music list's Collapse, the record's row order, the timer's choices and setting one, the ready sentence and the card in its place, the menu's heading, "Your link", the bar's line; the server list merged over this browser's, kept, and the browser's when the server doesn't answer; POST once after 30 s per session, not for a station stopped sooner).

**After the independent review** (one Medium, three Lows; each fix has a regression test that failed before it):

1. **This browser's copy of Stations is per profile.** It was one shared key, so a second profile on the same browser could see the first one's stations (the server's list, merged in, carries every device of that profile). The copy is now `ssRadioRecentStations:<profile id>` (from the shell's profile context), nothing is kept while no profile is known, the old shared key is removed when the tray starts, and a profile switch (`ss:webui-profile-context-changed`) redraws from that profile's copy and reads its server list again; an answer that arrives after a switch is ignored. Code: `radio.recent.ts` (`recentStationsKey`, `dropSharedRecent`), `player-radio.ts`. Tests: `player-radio.tray.test.ts` ("kept per profile"), `radio.recent.test.ts`.
2. **A link removed while it plays isn't written back.** `POST /api/radio/recent` looks a `link-` station up again (`resolve_station`) instead of using the hub's copy, so a deleted link answers 404 `not_found` and leaves no row. Other stations still come from the hub's copy, so a directory station isn't looked up again. Test: `test_radio_recent.py`.
3. **`radio_recent_v1` is recorded only by its own step.** It was also in `_KNOWN_MIGRATION_SIGNALS`, whose ledger back-fill would have recorded it from the table alone after a later part of the step (the index) failed; that entry is removed. Test: `test_radio_recent_migration.py` (the index's name taken by a table).
4. **The stream ends even without the server.** When the Sleep timer ends a station and the radio session isn't there to stop it, the player drops the stream itself (pause, remove `src`, `load()`), so no relay stays open; and the radio session's `stop()` now stops the player before it tells the server, so a server that doesn't answer can't keep a direct stream playing. Code: `media-player.js` `npFireSleepTimer`, `radio-session.ts` `stop`. Tests: `media-player-sleep-radio.test.ts`, `radio-session.test.ts`.

**Screenshots** (1440×900 and 390×844, in the session scratchpad's `shots-24b1/` beside the board renders `board-16a`–`board-16e`; not in the repo): the tray on Heard today and on Stations (this browser's list merged with the server's), the Sleep timer's choices and running, the "…" menu, the scan listening, found nothing and failed, 9c's confirm, the bar at 1440 and 1920, the Visuals transport, the phone sheet and mini bar. The player is the visual harness's rendering with a generated stream; the built shell on a scratch server (a copy of phase A's database, which upgraded with `radio_recent` and no errors) loads Home, Settings, Library, Listening history, Tracks, Radio, Stats, Wanted and Activity with a clean console.

### 2026-10-24: Dev, phase B2 (DJ stage 1 as drawn: key colours, energy bars, the docked Mixes panel)

Built on `ux/oct24-b2` from `integration` (f492a490), on Ross's yes, from `DJ stage 1 (Tracks, Mixes well with).dc.html` (17a–17e), the hand-off's key colours ("Added 2026-10-24") and Design's answers 150–154, with Dev's answers 164 (the panel lives in the shell) and 168 (`/browse` gains `keys`). Routes changed (no new ones): `/api/library/dj/browse` answers `keys`, `/api/library/dj/mixes/<id>` takes `within`, `POST /api/library/dj/start` takes `{track_id}` (`CONTRACTS.md`, "DJ data"). Patterns: `DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` (KeyChip, EnergyMeter, CamelotWheel in the components table; "Library → Tracks", "Mixes well with", "DJ data on a track row"). **Gauntlet:** nothing on `.github/gauntlet-paths.txt` was touched (`api/dj.py` and `core/dj/` aren't on it).

**Per board**

- **Key colours (151).** `features/dj/key-colour.ts` works out the hue, h = (n − 8) × 30° (8A and 8B at 0°, 7A at 330°, 1A at 150°); `KeyChip` (`key-chip.tsx`) draws it as the 6px chip (fill `oklch(0.7 0.12 h / 0.16)`, text `oklch(0.86 0.1 h)`, 14/600, `2px 6px`) where key is a column, the select or the panel, or as text in its hue at 600 in a meta line (17e: History, Search, the Artist page's Tracks tab). An uncertain key has no colour: "8B?" in meta, titled "Maybe F major (F), key uncertain" (17e's title). The hue rides in `--dj-key-h` (`data-key-hue` for tests).
- **Energy (151).** `EnergyMeter` (`energy-meter.tsx`): the number (15px tabular, 18px right-aligned) and ten 4 × 12px steps 2px apart, lit at 86%, the rest at 14%; "–" with none. Tracks only.
- **Tracks (17a, 17c).** Header copy and the 280px "Filter by title or artist" beside the title; the analysis line with its 120px bar and Stop / Start now as accent-light words; the toolbar: BPM From–To (72px), Key drawn as 17a's button (the chip, "A minor", the chevron; the native select lies over it, transparent, so the list and keyboard are the browser's), "And keys that mix", "Sort: BPM" as a ghost button the same way; the count line "541 tracks at 120–128 BPM in 8A, 7A, 9A or 8B · Clear filters" from `/browse`'s `total` and `keys` (`dj.format.ts` `filterCountLine`; "matching “q”", "at 120 BPM or more" and "at up to 128 BPM" for the other filters); rows `48px 1fr 64px 64px 96px 92px` at gap 12 under a 36px head, BPM right-aligned, the key chip, the energy bar, Play (accent) and a borderless "…" on hover; the playing track's row in a 10% accent wash (17a's first row). Phone (17c): no page title (the top bar has it) and no bar; the line without its "BPM, key and energy:" lead; BPM's label and two inputs in a grid; the key button and "And keys that mix" on one row; Sort full width; rows `44px 1fr 44px 44px` with "124 BPM · **8A** · E7 · artist" and two bordered buttons, the "…" opening the action sheet in 154's order.
- **Mixes well with (17a, 17b, 152, 164).** The 680 dialog (`mixes-dialog.tsx`) is gone. `MixesPanelHost` (`features/dj/mixes-panel.tsx`) renders beside the router's outlet in `routes/__root.tsx`, so the panel stays open across pages; a small store (`mixes-store.ts`: `openMixes`, `closeMixes`, Follow, Widen) replaces `useMixesWith()`, and the open seed is kept per tab (`sessionStorage` `soulsync.dj.mixes.seed`) so a link that loads a whole page (the rows' artist and album links) keeps it open too. Docked at ≥ 1100px: fixed between the top bar and the player (`--ss-topbar-h`, `--ss-player-h`), 400px on `#101010` with a hairline left, and `html[data-mixes-panel="docked"]` gives the page column `margin-right: 400px` (and lifts `style.css`'s 960px floor on `.main-content`, which otherwise kept the page under the panel); the floating "?" and bell and the toasts move 424px in. 769–1099px: the same panel over the page with a shadow (question 193). Inside: the label, the title at 18/600, "Tidewater · 124 BPM · A minor"; the 148px `CamelotWheel` beside the rule in two lines ("Same key, one step round the wheel, or its relative major." / "Within 6% of 124 BPM, or half and double time."); "Follow what's playing" (on; remembered per browser in `soulsync.dj.mixes.follow`); rows `40px 1fr 44px 44px` (key chip `0 5px`, BPM, how it mixes at 72%; Play next, Add to playlist, borderless); the foot: "572 tracks mix with it" and the secondary "Make a playlist from these…". A lit wheel segment is a button ("Show tracks in 9A"): it sets Tracks' Key filter (17b's note).
- **Follow what's playing.** The host listens to the player's `ss:player`; when the loaded track changes to a library track (not a station, episode or book) it asks `/mixes` for it and re-seeds only if the library knows it, so a stream from elsewhere leaves the panel as it is.
- **The states (17b).** Loading: "Finding tracks that mix…". Not analysed: "SYNCHRO.S hasn't worked out this track's key yet. It's in the queue (412 left); analyse it now." (the link for admins: `POST /api/library/dj/start {track_id}` puts it first, or next in a running sweep; then "Working it out now…" and the panel looks again every 4 s); a skipped track says why; one analysed without a key says there's nothing to match. Key uncertain: "Its key sounded like 8B, but SYNCHRO.S isn't sure, so these matches may not mix." above the rows, and the wheel outlines 8B in its hue instead of filling it. Nothing matches: "Nothing in your library mixes with it within 6%. Widen to 10%" (`within=10`; the rule's second line follows). Gone from the library (404): one sentence. A failure: the danger Notice with Try again.
- **Make a playlist from these (17b).** A 480px dialog: "Make a playlist from these", Name ("Mixes with Night Ferry"), "Start with Night Ferry" (on), Cancel and the primary "Make playlist (7)" (the rows plus the seed). It makes the playlist through `POST /api/user-playlists` (Add to playlist's "New playlist" route); the toast "Made Mixes with Night Ferry with 7 tracks" carries **Open it**, which opens the playlist (`openMirroredPlaylistModal`; `showToast` gained `opts.action`).
- **Phone (17d).** The sheet: `#141416` from 96px down over a 50% scrim, the grabber, the header (17/600, "Tidewater · 124 BPM · **8A** A minor" with the key's text in its hue), rows `44px 1fr 44px 44px` with "8A · Same key · −1.6%" and bordered buttons, then a 48px "Make a playlist from these…". No wheel and no Follow, as drawn (question 191).
- **Other rows (17e, 154).** `DjMeta` puts the key's text in its hue; no energy. The orders match 154 everywhere: History's sheet and "…", Tracks' "…" and sheet (Play next, Mixes well with…, Add to playlist…, Open artist, Open album), the Artist page's Tracks tab (Mixes well with… straight after Play next). Search's library track rows had no "…": they gain a ghost "Mixes well with…" beside Play (question 194). Albums' tiles get nothing.
- **Checkbox.** The shared `Checkbox` takes the 6px radius every board draws (17a, 14a, 21a; it was 4px).

**Decisions**

1. The wheel's neighbours are worked out in TypeScript (`mixKeys`, the server's `compatible_keys` rule), since the wheel lights them before any answer; the count line still names the keys `/browse` says it covered.
2. The panel's seed survives a full page load per tab (sessionStorage), not across tabs or restarts; Follow's choice is per browser.
3. Docking starts at 1100px; below it the panel lies over the page; on a phone (≤ 768px, the shell's own phone width) it's the sheet.
4. "Make playlist (N)" counts the loaded rows (the first 50) plus the seed.

**Questions for Design (190–198)**

190. Brief and notes call "Follow what's playing" a switch; 17a draws a checkbox. Built as the checkbox (20px, 6px radius, accent-light). Fine?
191. 17d has no wheel and no Follow. Built as drawn, but Follow still applies on a phone: a new track re-seeds the sheet while it's open. Show the checkbox there, or turn following off on phones?
192. 17c draws no page title, no filter field and no count line. Built: the title hidden (the top bar names the page), the filter field kept (as History's phone keeps its field) and the count line kept, since it holds Clear filters. Fine?
193. Between 769 and 1099px the panel lies over the page with a shadow instead of docking (docking would leave the page under 450px). Draw that width?
194. Search's library track rows have Play only, no "…"; built a ghost "Mixes well with…" beside Play. Or a "…" with 154's order?
195. "analyse it now": after the press the line ends "Working it out now…" until the key arrives. Members (who can't start the analysis) read "It's in the queue (412 left)." without the link. Words fine?
196. "Make playlist (N)" counts the rows loaded (up to 50, plus the seed) while the foot can say "572 tracks mix with it". Cap the playlist at 50, or ask for up to 200?
197. The words for a seed analysis skipped ("SYNCHRO.S can't work out this track's key: too long to analyse."), one analysed without a key ("…couldn't work out this track's key, so there's nothing to match it against.") and one gone from the library ("That track isn't in your library any more, so there's nothing to match.") aren't drawn.
198. The shared Checkbox went from 4px to 6px corners app-wide, as every board draws it. Confirm.

Web: `npm run check` (0 errors), `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`; the full vitest suite with `--testTimeout=60000`: 651 files, 11,603 tests; 11,599 passed and 4 in files this branch doesn't touch (the Mirror dialog, Setup check, Wanted's nebula, the audiobook wishlist) timed out while the build and the screenshots ran beside it, and pass when run again. New: `key-colour.test.ts` (the hue mapping, the wheel's keys), `dj-pieces.test.tsx` (KeyChip, EnergyMeter, the wheel's neighbours, buttons and the uncertain outline), `mixes-panel.test.tsx` (the panel's parts; surviving navigation and a page load; the wheel setting Tracks' key; Follow what's playing re-seeding, ignoring a stream the library doesn't know and a station, and staying put when off; Make a playlist through the playlist route with Open it; every 17b state; the phone sheet), `tracks-page.test.tsx` (the chips' hues, the energy bar, the count line and Clear filters, the panel from the "…").

### 2026-10-24: Dev, phase A of 24 October, and answers 160–168

Built on `ux/oct24-a` from `integration` (09432d0f), from Phase A of Design's "Build order for Dev, 24 October" and the boards `Radio tray (expanded player).dc.html` (16a–16e), `DJ stage 1 (Tracks, Mixes well with).dc.html` (17a–17e), `Answers 100-134.dc.html` (18a–20b) and `Move to another drive.dc.html` (14i), with Design's answers in `Start here - Design notes 2026-10-24.dc.html`. No routes changed; `CONTRACTS.md` says what `/api/library/dj/browse` and the Heard counts already give these screens ("DJ data", "Internet radio"); the patterns moved into `DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` (the tray in radio mode, the bar, the Visuals overlay, Scan now services, the drive-away Notice, the Folders card, the Docker step, Listening history, Library → Tracks, `ActionSheet`). **Nothing on `.github/gauntlet-paths.txt` was touched** (web files only: no `core/drives/**`, no `api/*`, no `database/**`), so this branch needs no gauntlet review.

"See the lines it adds" no longer repeats it. Code: `features/drives/docker-step.tsx` `HelperSteps`.

Done is counted over the library (`counts.tracks − counts.pending`, so skipped and failed tracks count as done), not the sweep's batch. Finished: "All 3,740 tracks have BPM, key and energy", no bar, for a day after the sweep (`job.recent`), then nothing. The other states' words are question 173. - **154** "Mixes well with…" is second everywhere: History's phone sheet and desktop "…" and Tracks' "…" read Play next, Mixes well with…, Add to playlist…, Open artist, Open album (both desktop menus gained Play next first); the Artist page's Tracks tab keeps its Play and Add to queue and puts Mixes well with… straight after Play next.

**Components changed**

- `ActionSheet` (`routes/library/-ui/action-sheet.tsx`): a row can be `disabled` with a `reason` (62% text, no hover, `aria-disabled`, the reason in meta at the row's end; choosing it does nothing and keeps the sheet).
- The Visuals overlay's transport (`index.html`) gains the radio pieces (Live and time listened, Stop); `player.ts` swaps them by source kind.
- The Docker step's list is a component of its own (`HelperSteps`).
- Settings → Radio's automatic AudD state line takes `data-tone` `warning | danger | neutral` with a CSS dot.

**Design's questions for Dev (160–168), answered**

- **160. Can the drive helper report each mount's host side? Yes, cheaply, without a new credential.** The helper already parses every service's volumes as (source, target) on the computer itself (`compose_tool.service_info`), so it knows `./music` → `/app/Transfer` and can resolve `./music` against the compose folder to `/Users/ross/SoulSync/music`; on a Mac it can name the volume (`diskutil info -plist <path>`, `VolumeName`, "Macintosh HD"; `df -P` on Linux). What it can't do is call SYNCHRO.S back: its one-use token is spent fetching the script, and a second token in the command adds a credential to the shell history. So the proposal: **the helper writes `.synchros-host.json` beside the marker SYNCHRO.S already keeps at each music folder's root** (`{"host_path", "volume", "marker_id", "written_at"}`), through the mount itself, and SYNCHRO.S reads it in the marker watch and trusts it only while `marker_id` matches the folder's own `.synchros-folder.json` (a compose file edited later, or a folder pointed elsewhere, drops it). A new `sh drives-helper.sh --names` refreshes it without changing the compose file, for installs that ran the helper before this lands. Two cases need nothing: `/Volumes:/Volumes` mounts are the same path inside and out, so after a move to Music HD the Remove list and Traktor's paths already speak the Mac's names; and Windows (by-hand steps, no helper) keeps container paths with "on D:" until the PowerShell twin. Cost: about 40 lines in `compose_tool.py` (the helper), 30 in `core/drives/markers.py` and `api/drives.py` (`folders` and the move's rows gain `host_path`), tests with the existing compose fixtures; **through the gauntlet** (`core/drives/**`, `api/drives.py`). Recommendation: build it in Phase C with 125, sidecar not environment variable (an env line is another compose edit and a restart for every refresh).
- **161. Measuring a month's downloads by size.** Two ways. (a) **Now, no schema:** `track_downloads` already records each imported download's `source_size` and `created_at` (written by the import pipeline, `core/imports/side_effects.py`), so `SUM(source_size)` over 30 days is "about 22 GB of downloads this month" today. It counts what came in, not what fills the disk: an upgrade that replaces a file adds its whole size though the disk grows by the difference, and a source that doesn't report a size writes 0. (b) **The honest figure for "fills in about seven weeks": a daily sample of the drive** (used bytes from `disks.usage` and the music folders' sizes the Folders card already measures) in a small table, `drive_usage_daily (day, drive, used_bytes, music_bytes)`, one row a day from the drive watch; the rate is the 30-day slope of `used_bytes`, which includes upgrades, deletions and anything else writing to the drive, and the line waits until 14 days are kept. Cost: (a) a query and a route field, half a day; (b) a migration (**gauntlet**: `database/**`), a daily write in `core/drives/markers.py` (**gauntlet**), the line and its tests, about a day. Recommendation: (b), with (a)'s figure as the words if Design wants "downloads" named ("At this month's rate (about 22 GB of downloads)…").
- **162. Deck B to headphones with `setSinkId`.** `HTMLMediaElement.setSinkId()` sends one `<audio>` element to a chosen output: Chrome and Edge have had it for years, Firefox since 116, **Safari only since 18.4** (macOS 15.4, 2025; per caniuse's data, not tested here: this sandbox has no Mac). `AudioContext.setSinkId()` (a whole Web Audio graph to one output) is **Chrome and Edge only** (110+); not Safari, not Firefox. Choosing a non-default output needs the person's permission: Chrome lists `audiooutput` devices once the page has microphone permission (or after `selectAudioOutput()` in Firefox); how Safari asks is untested. So one tab *can* play Deck A to the speakers and Deck B to headphones: in Chrome with two AudioContexts each `setSinkId`'d (keeps the EQ and crossfader in Web Audio), and in Safari 18.4+ by ending Deck B's graph in a `MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode` played by an `<audio>` element that is `setSinkId`'d (works wherever the element method does; adds 20–50 ms of latency to the cue, fine for previewing, not for beat-matching by ear across both). Fallbacks where neither exists (older Safari, or one output only): **split cue**, the DJ-software standard: master summed to mono in the left channel, the cue in the right, on one output through a Y-splitter or a headphone-only listen (one ChannelMergerNode, works in every browser); or a 4-channel interface, sending the cue to channels 3–4 (`destination.maxChannelCount` ≥ 4; Chrome honours it, Safari reports 2 on many devices, untested). Recommendation: build Deck B's preview on the element route (one code path for Chrome, Firefox and Safari 18.4+), detect `'setSinkId' in HTMLMediaElement.prototype`, offer split cue otherwise; a Settings choice with a test tone (question 175). Check on Ross's Mac before stage 3 starts.
- **163. Recent stations per profile on the server: a table.** Profile settings are columns on `profiles` (accent, home page, ListenBrainz…), each a scalar read with the profile on every request; a list of eight stations with their names and logos would be a JSON blob there, rewritten whole from two devices at once. The radio tables already have the shape: `radio_favourites (profile_id, station_id, station_json, created_at)`. So `radio_recent (profile_id, station_id, station_json, played_at, PRIMARY KEY (profile_id, station_id))`, trimmed to eight per profile on write, written by the server when a session has played 30 s (the relay knows; for direct mode the page sends one `POST /api/radio/recent`), read by `GET /api/radio/recent`; `localStorage` stays the first paint. Cost: a migration (**gauntlet**, `database/**`), two routes, the tray reading them, about half a day.
- **164. "Mixes well with" as a docked panel in the shell: yes.** The router's root (`routes/__root.tsx`) already renders hosts beside `<Outlet />` that live across every page (`IssueDomainHost`, `AutoSyncHost`), and every page that opens it (History, the Artist page, Search, Tracks) is a React route under that root. So a `MixesPanelHost` there, with a small store (`openMixes(seed)`, `close`, `follow`) replacing today's per-page `useMixesWith()` dialog, survives navigation; "Follow what's playing" listens to the player's `ss:player` event, which the shell fires on every page. The page column makes room by a `data-mixes-panel` attribute on the shell root (`padding-right: 400px` on the content, the player bar stays full width under both, as 17a draws); a phone gets the sheet (17d) from the same host. Cost: about a day (the store and host, the 400px panel from the dialog's rows, the shell's layout rule, moving the four openers); no routes. Recommendation: build it in the shell in Phase B.
- **165. The per-station "AudD every track" flag: on the station, not the subscription.** `radio_auto_add` is per profile (each member's own auto-add), but AudD's requests are the install's (one token, one daily cap, one captured stream per station shared by everyone), so the flag belongs to the station: a small admin-only table `radio_station_settings (station_id PRIMARY KEY, audd_every_track, changed_by, changed_at)` (custom links could carry it as a column, built-in stations have no row of their own). It applies while the station is watched, as automatic AudD does now (someone playing it, or any profile's auto-add on it). **Heard can give tracks an hour:** `radio_heard` has `(station_id, heard_at)` indexed and keeps a row per track window, identified or not (the not-identified rows are windows too, with `duration_s`). The rate is distinct track windows over the hours that have any row, over the last 7 days (rows are per profile, so windows are counted once per station across profiles by `track_key`, or by `heard_at` within 60 s for unnamed ones). The catch: Heard is sparse for exactly the stations that need AudD (nothing names their tracks, so only windows the fingerprint tried are rows), so the sentence uses the station's own rate when it has 3+ listened hours and 10+ rows, else "about 15 an hour" (Design's figure), and says which. Cost: a migration (**gauntlet**), the switch and its confirm, the rate query, the hub's check; about a day.
- **166. Can SYNCHRO.S tell from `collection.nml` alone that Traktor is open? No, not reliably enough to refuse a write.** Confident of (Native Instruments' manual and support articles): Traktor Pro 3 keeps the collection in memory and **writes `collection.nml` when it closes** (and when someone right-clicks Track Collection → Save Collection), and on a close after changes it writes a timestamped backup into `Backup/Collection/` in Traktor's root (`Collection_YYYYyMMmDDd_HHhMMmSSs.nml`). So while Traktor runs the file's modified time is the *last* close's, which looks exactly like Traktor being closed. Not confident of: any lock file: none is documented in what Dev found, and Dev can't check (no Traktor here); and whether Traktor re-reads the file while running (it isn't documented to; the assumption has to be that it doesn't, and overwrites the file on close). Mixed In Key's Traktor integration waits for Traktor to close by watching the *process* on the same Mac, which SYNCHRO.S in Docker can't see. **Recommended rule for 21d:** (1) the person says Traktor is closed (the confirm's check), (2) right before writing, SYNCHRO.S re-reads `collection.nml` and refuses if its size, modified time or SHA-256 differ from what the preview read (Traktor saved in between, so it was open or reopened), (3) it writes a named copy first, then the new file to a temporary name and renames it over (never in place), (4) afterwards it watches the file for the week "Put Traktor's file back" lasts: if Traktor writes it again and SYNCHRO.S's changes are gone, it says so ("Traktor was open and wrote its own copy; nothing of yours was lost: apply the changes again with Traktor closed") rather than guessing earlier. A host-side check could make step 1 real: the drives helper could gain `--traktor` that reports `pgrep -x Traktor` through the same sidecar as 160, but a one-off check is only as fresh as its last run, so it can't replace step 2. Write-back stays last and through the gauntlet.
- **167. Space in the overlay while a station plays: Stop, built (145).** Nothing else binds Space there: the overlay's own keys (`media-player-v2.js` `npv2HandleKeys`: V, Shift V, R, S, L, F, Esc, ?) and the visuals' (`visuals.logic.ts` `overlayKey`) don't take it, so it reaches the player's handler (`media-player.js`), which now stops a station and still plays or pauses anything else; a focused control (the panel's rows, its switches) keeps its own Space, as before.
- **168. Does `/api/library/dj/tracks` return the count line's total? No, but `/browse` does.** `/tracks` is a lookup by ids for other pages' rows. `/browse` returns `total` for the whole filter (the BPM range, the key or `8A:mix`, `q`, in the caller's library) and echoes the filter as read (`bpm_min`, `bpm_max`, `key`), so "38 tracks at 120–128 BPM" needs nothing new. What it doesn't return is what `:mix` expanded to ("in 8A, 7A, 9A or 8B"); the rule lives in `core/dj/camelot.py` `compatible_keys`, so Phase B adds `keys` to `/browse`'s answer (one line, `api/dj.py`, not a gauntlet path) rather than repeating the wheel in TypeScript.

**Decided by Dev**

1. **The "restarts the stream" note went** with 105's sentence (question 170).
2. **The stopped line's text is secondary** in every tone, the dot carrying it, as 20a draws (question 171).
3. **Play waits for the drive in the menus and the sheet too**, not only the row's Play (question 172).
4. **Done counts the library**, not the running sweep, and includes tracks that couldn't be analysed, so the bar fills (question 173).
5. **148 by measuring**, with the 1180px media rule removed; the meter's own under-1180 rule (it may shrink to 48px) stays.
6. **Space stops a station everywhere**, not only in the overlay: the bar has no Pause either, and one key doing two things on two screens would surprise.
